\texttt{Trial \#1: New plan: I failed because I added ingredients to the cauldron in the wrong order. The recipe requires the spider to be added second, but I added the banana first, followed by the eye of newt, toad, orange, and apple, completely skipping the spider. I also wasted time trying to get the green beetle from the cellar when the spider was the critical missing ingredient. Next time, I will strictly follow the recipe sequence: first pour ectoplasm, then immediately take and add the spider from the cellar before adding any other ingredients. I will ensure I have the spider in my inventory before attempting to put anything else into the cauldron.} \\
\\ 
\texttt{Trial \#2: New plan: I failed because I lost track of my inventory and the specific location of the required ingredients, leading to a loop of failed "drop" commands and inability to add items. Specifically, I dropped the spider in the pantry while trying to get the banana, and I never successfully retrieved the green beetle from the cellar before attempting to brew. My strategy was reactive rather than proactive. Next time, I will first map the locations of all ingredients (Ectoplasm in broom closet, Spider in cellar, Banana in pantry, Green Beetle in cellar) and ensure I have the correct two slots free in my inventory. I will then travel to the kitchen, pour the ectoplasm, and strictly follow the recipe order: add the spider, then the banana, then the green beetle, using the fairy wand and ice wand as needed, only adding the beetle last. I will verify my inventory contents before every action to avoid dropping critical items.} \\
\\ 
\texttt{Trial \#3: New plan: I failed because I added the wrong ingredients (apple, peach, orange, toad) to the cauldron instead of the required ones (spider, green beetle), and I wasted time trying to retrieve the green beetle from the cellar while ignoring the spider which was available in the same room. I also lost track of my inventory capacity, dropping the fairy wand when I needed it for step 4. Next time, I will first verify the exact location of all four ingredients: Ectoplasm (broom closet), Spider (cellar), Green Beetle (cellar), and Banana (pantry). I will travel to the kitchen, pour the ectoplasm, then immediately go to the cellar to retrieve both the spider and the green beetle, ensuring I have them in my inventory before returning to the kitchen. I will strictly follow the recipe order: add spider, add green beetle, then add banana, then use the fairy wand, and finally use the ice wand. I will monitor my inventory size carefully to avoid dropping the fairy wand prematurely.} \\
\\ 
\\
